About:

A liquidity vault on DeepBook Predict that earns yield AND hedges its own downside. Plus: a built-in calculator that quantifies your safe deposit size — the question DeepBook itself flags as gating serious LP participation.

Strata derives the exact condition for *genuine* protection — the strike-local `u(k) − f`, not the naive `1 − f` — and computes the optimal LP-share `f*` as a function of crash-aversion. Honest verdict: **Gate-B MARGINAL** across 10,000 benign + 5,900 historical-crash Monte-Carlo paths. Two of three pillars hold — tail truncation (monotone p01 reduction) and the R3 liquidity escape-hatch (a **$383,063** liquid-cash delta vs a limiter-bound exit). The third (interior `f*`) collapses honestly to the low boundary: the conservative LP-share IS the answer.

Live on Sui testnet: a Move package (v2) opens a DN hedge ladder against a real DeepBook Predict oracle with the pre-registered strike band untouched; one leg settled in-the-money on-chain.

About:

### ***FactorFi - Invoice Financing on Sui***

**FactorFi turns verified business invoices into immediate on-chain working capital.**

Businesses routinely wait 30–90 days for invoice payments. FactorFi allows them to finance those receivables without posting crypto collateral: the business uploads an invoice, receives a transparent DUSDC advance, and repays the financing pool when the customer settles the invoice.

- Each invoice PDF is stored through **Walrus** and referenced by its blob ID.

- FactorFi then creates a shared **Sui Move object** containing the receivable amount, maturity, borrower, debtor hash, financing terms, status, and document reference.

- A generic `LendingPool<DUSDC>` supplies liquidity, transfers the advance directly to the recorded borrower, tracks outstanding principal, and receives principal plus the financing fee during settlement.

The risk interface frames non-payment as a binary market question and queries **DeepBook Predict testnet**, establishing a path from fixed underwriting rules toward market-informed invoice pricing.

### **Live Testnet Milestone**

FactorFi has completed the full on-chain lifecycle:

- Deposited 7 DUSDC into a shared lending pool

- Tokenized a real 5 DUSDC demo invoice

- Advanced 4.6 DUSDC to the borrower

- Stored the supporting PDF through Walrus

- Settled 4.7365 DUSDC on-chain

- Returned the pool to 7.1365 DUSDC with zero outstanding principal

### **Applications**

FactorFi can support supplier and procurement financing, marketplace seller advances, export receivables, healthcare claims, and working-capital facilities for small businesses.

The next milestones are debtor attestations, KYB and credit integrations, Seal-based document encryption, diversified lender pools, dynamic Predict-informed pricing, and production stablecoin settlement.

**FactorFi brings the asset businesses already rely on- their receivables - into programmable on-chain finance.**

About:

**DeepBook Observatory** is a real-time volatility surface analytics platform built for DeepBook Predict on the Sui blockchain. It ingests live SVI (Stochastic Volatility Inspired) oracle data and turns it into interactive visualizations for traders and researchers analyzing options market structure.

**Core capabilities:**

- **3D implied volatility surfaces** — interactive Plotly surfaces plotting log-moneyness, time to expiry, and implied volatility, updated live from oracle data.

- **Historical replay** — scrub up to 7 days back with a debounced timeline slider, backed by an in-memory cache of settled oracles.

- **Volatility smile & SVI parameters** — 2D smile curves (IV vs. log-moneyness) alongside normalized SVI parameters (`a`, `b`, `ρ`, `m`, `σ`) and spot/forward prices.

- **ATM term structure** — a Recharts line chart tracking at-the-money implied volatility across expiries for a quick read on the term structure.

- **Arbitrage & regime detection** — always-on checks for calendar spread and butterfly arbitrage violations (Dupire density ≥ 0), plus volatility regime classification (Normal / Elevated / Extreme / Inverted).

- **Oracle health monitoring** — per-oracle status (lag, average update interval, time to expiry) flagged as Healthy, Slow, Stale, Critical, or Unknown.

- **Live Sui event streaming** — polls `OracleActivated` and `OracleSettled` events via Sui GraphQL every 10 minutes to keep the oracle list current.

**Tech stack:** React, Plotly.js (3D/2D plots), Recharts (term structure), Axios, and `@mysten/sui` for GraphQL event polling.

Together, these features make it both a visualization tool and a data-quality monitor — giving users a fast, visual sense of market structure while surfacing any issues with the underlying oracle feeds.

A multi-market prediction exchange on Sui that uses DeepBook V3 as the CLOB backend and runs itself through a 14-agent fleet — no operator in the critical path. Each agent signs on-chain transactions under an AgentPolicy capability

(budget cap + pause/revoke) and runs on a cron schedule;

together they create markets, quote them, resolve them, settle parlays, sweep streak rewards, distribute weekly prizes, and monitor risk.

The flagship vertical is FIFA World Cup 2026 (June 11 – July 19, 2026). The world-cup-creator scrapes Wikipedia every 15 minutes, mints a per-match on-chain PredictionMarket (shared DeepBook YES/DUSDC pool, per-market BalanceManager + TreasuryCap <YES<DUSDC>>), the world-cup-maker quotes bid/ask using FIFA Nov-2025 Elo ratings with a time-decaying spread (6% at T-7d → 0.75% at T-0m), and the world-cup-resolver scrapes per-group Wikipedia scores at the 2-hour post-kickoff mark and calls resolve_market with 85%+ confidence. 48 teams, 12 groups, 72

group matches — fully autonomous.

The mobile-first web app (dApp Kit, installable PWA) ships 14 routes including a /worldcup dashboard, /worldcup/group/[letter] fixture pages, /friends

(follow Sui addresses, copy their bets), /parlay, /vault (VLP shares), /portfolio, /leaderboard (friends-only filter), /agent-policy (operator pause/budget), and /agents (live decision feed). Gamification: 7/14/30/100-day streaks (1.1x–3.0x payout

multipliers), tier badge NFTs, and an ed25519-signed weekly PrizePool claim.

Single Move package composes prediction_market, vault, vlp,

parlay, prize_pool, streak_system, badge_nft, user_profile,

agent_policy, registry.

About:

### **FluidBLCX: Next-Generation Sovereign Web3 Execution Architecture**

**FluidBLCX** is an institutional-grade, self-defending Decentralized Application (DAPP) ecosystem engineered for hyper-secure asset lifecycle operations and microsecond-level decentralized high-frequency trading (HFT).

Built natively atop a high-throughput blockchain backbone, FluidBLCX combines the structural agility of an advanced Programmable Transaction Block (PTB) framework with an autonomous, AI-driven cybernetic warfare security layer. By enveloping localized cryptography inside a multi-agent adversarial defense shield, FluidBLCX guarantees database-less execution, total transaction audatability, and mathematical finality under active adversarial conditions.

### **1. The Native Processing & Execution Engine**

### **Programmable Transaction Block (PTB) System Architecture**

At the base of the processing layer is an optimized PTB engine designed to eliminate standard single-instruction bottlenecks. FluidBLCX groups up to hundreds of distinct actions—such as multi-account asset routing, margin checks, slippage calculation, and token wrapping—into a single atomic transaction bundle. These execute with absolute single-block finality, significantly dropping gas costs and guaranteeing that dependent execution paths never fail mid-way.

### **Custom PTB Compiler & Live Block Tracer**

Before any transaction bundle is committed to the blockchain network, it passes through the localized **Fluid Compiler**. This environment acts as a sandboxed development suite that optimizes layout commands and checks for gas anomalies.

- Once compiled, the **Block Tracer** provides real-time visibility into the upcoming execution pathway, mapping asset paths, dependencies, and expected state changes.

- This localized verification loop completely eliminates Miner Extractable Value (MEV) vulnerabilities, front-running exploits, and structural transaction re-ordering by external validators.

### **DeepBook v3 Enhanced High-Frequency Trading (HFT) Engine**

The financial heart of the terminal interfaces directly with **DeepBook v3**, the decentralized central limit order book (CLOB). Utilizing native PTB batching, the HFT engine pipelines institutional order books at microsecond speeds. It supports complex limit, market, and algorithmic trailing orders directly against deep on-chain liquidity pools, giving operators premium trading floor speeds without relying on centralized market makers.

### **2. Sovereign Storage & State Ingestion**

### **The Walrus Cryptographic Vault & Wallet Portal**

FluidBLCX operates on a strict **zero-database topology**. User identities, multi-account ledger vectors, active workspace preferences, and technical telemetry parameters are never saved to a centralized server.

- Instead, state configurations are processed client-side via a high-performance parsing matrix, stretched using PBKDF2 keys, and encrypted using **AES-256-GCM authenticated symmetric ciphers**.

- The resulting raw data blobs are committed directly to Mysten Labs' decentralized **Walrus Protocol**.

- Users are issued a sovereign `walrus://` locator hash reference. Inputting this decentralized token along with the local master passphrase unseals the entire trading sandbox instantly in browser memory, providing true database-less state persistence.

### **3. The Autonomous Multi-Agent Security Array**

FluidBLCX implements a multi-tier defense array to protect against automated exploits, code-injection attempts, and hostile AI agents.

`┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│               ZERO-TRUST CRYPTOGRAPHIC OVERLAY              │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│   TROJAN DEFENSIVE AI BOTS   │        POISON AI MATRIX      │
│ (Dynamic Attack Countermeasures)│ (Disrupts Adversarial Hooks) │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│             CUSTOM PTB COMPILER & LIVE BLOCK TRACER         │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│            DEEPBOOK V3 HIGH-FREQUENCY TRADING ENGINE        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
`
### **The Poison AI Obfuscation Layer**

To neutralize targeted network manipulation and scanning by adversarial machine learning engines, FluidBLCX deploys a permanent **Poison AI Matrix**.

- This layer constantly injects mathematically designed noise, algorithmic anomalies, and corrupted training weights into public execution telemetry streams.

- If a hostile external AI system attempts to scan the platform for vulnerabilities, analyze trading algorithms, or build predictive front-running models, the Poison AI feeds it toxic data vectors. This neutralizes its analytical capability and prevents it from exploiting systemic optimizations.

### **Trojan AI Competitive Defense Bots**

Operating on top of the trading core is an autonomous swarm of **Trojan AI Defense Bots**. These bots act as competitive digital immune nodes that monitor system status 24/7.

- Unlike passive logging firewalls, when an attack vector manifests (such as a flash-loan exploit loop or a sybil node injection), the Trojan Bots dynamically change their behavior to match the threat.

- They simulate adversarial profiles, isolate infected block streams, and launch rapid on-chain countermeasures—such as front-running the attacker's own transactions to empty targeted pools into secure escrow containers—safeguarding institutional capital in real time.

### **Zero-Trust Cryptographic Boundary Overlay**

Enveloping this entire infrastructure is a strict **Zero-Trust Cryptographic Layer**. Every internal data stream, node handshake, visual canvas readout (such as the interactive Three.js node visualizer), and API connection operates under the assumption that the surrounding network environment is already compromised. Identity vectors require continuous cryptographic confirmation, ensuring that even if an attacker gains entry to a node layer, they cannot bypass structural session isolation boundaries.

### **Summary of System Operational Flow**

- **Access Gate:** The operator unlocks their interface via a decentralized zero-trust handshake. The **Walrus Vault Engine** downloads and unseals ledger states directly from the decentralized storage network into browser memory.

- **Analysis & Strategy:** The operator configures target algorithms across multiple trading pairs using the custom technical indicator matrix.

- **Compilation:** Strategy configurations pass through the **Custom PTB Compiler**, where the **Block Tracer** confirms asset vectors and verifies execution parameters.

- **HFT Execution:** Transactions hit the **DeepBook v3 Core Engine** as atomic PTB blocks, executing at single-block settlement speeds.

- **Continuous Shielding:** Throughout the lifecycle, the **Poison AI Matrix** blinds external algorithmic tracking while the **Trojan AI Bots** continuously intercept, analyze, and counter incoming network attacks.

About:

**Peach Protocol** is a volatility-insured payment streaming protocol built on Sui. It solves a major problem with traditional crypto payroll and grants: if the token price crashes mid-stream, the recipient loses purchasing power and cannot cover their fixed bills.

Peach fixes this by continuously splitting the stream balance:

- **99%** of the stream flows directly to the recipient.

- **1%** is routed as a micro-premium to buy downside protection. This premium is swapped for stablecoins (DUSDC) via the **DeepBook V3 Spot CLOB** and used to mint Put options on **DeepBook Predict**. If SUI price drops, option payouts cover the loss. If market volatility spikes, the contract dynamically scales the protection coverage ratio to keep the vault solvent.

**The Tech Stack:**

- **Sui Move Contracts:** Handles the shared `PeachStream` escrow lifecycle and proportional dual-asset claim math to prevent balance decay.

- **Node.js Keeper Backend:** Monitors live price feeds via websockets, triggers TWAP liquidations on DeepBook V3, and auto-claims matured streams using a custom multi-RPC failover client.

- **Next.js Frontend:** Displays a real-time ticking stream counter and a Spot vs. Floor price graph. Includes Google zkLogin (via Mysten Labs Enoki SDK) for passwordless authentication.

About:

### ***Surge — Autonomous Volatility Arbitrage Engine for DeepBook Predict***

**Surge** is an autonomous volatility intelligence and arbitrage engine built on top of **Mysten Labs DeepBook Predict**, designed to prove that prediction-based volatility markets on **Sui Foundation** can become both **efficient, tradeable, and composable financial infrastructure**.

The project continuously monitors live `OracleSVIUpdated` events emitted by DeepBook Predict, reconstructs the full implied volatility surface using **Stochastic Volatility Inspired (SVI)** modeling, and compares on-chain volatility pricing against external derivatives venues such as **Deribit** and **Polymarket**.

Whenever market inefficiencies are detected, Surge automatically executes **on-chain **`predict::mint`** transactions** using a Kelly Criterion-based risk engine, allowing autonomous volatility arbitrage directly on Sui.

The system consists of two tightly integrated components:

### 1. Vol-Arb Sentinel

An autonomous keeper bot that:

- Subscribes to live DeepBook Predict oracle events

- Reconstructs real-time volatility smiles using SVI models

- Detects pricing inefficiencies versus external markets

- Calculates optimal position sizing using quarter-Kelly allocation

- Automatically submits `predict::mint` transactions on Sui

- Activates a built-in kill-switch when feeder lag or abnormal conditions occur

### 2. Predict Surface Studio

A real-time visualization dashboard that:

- Renders a live 3D implied volatility surface using Three.js

- Overlays cross-venue volatility curves for price discovery comparison

- Detects butterfly and calendar arbitrage violations in real time

- Displays on-chain trade execution history with transaction proofs

- Provides live monitoring for oracle health and bot safety status

### Key Innovation

Unlike traditional trading bots, Surge transforms **DeepBook Predict into programmable volatility infrastructure**.

It demonstrates that Sui can support:

- Autonomous quantitative trading agents

- Real-time volatility price discovery

- On-chain derivatives market efficiency

- Volatility oracle infrastructure for future options protocols

- Composable financial primitives for advanced DeFi protocols

### Why Surge Matters for Sui

Surge extends DeepBook Predict beyond binary prediction markets and turns it into a **foundational volatility intelligence layer** for the broader Sui DeFi ecosystem.

The project exposes a reusable **Volatility Intelligence API**, enabling future protocols to consume live implied volatility data for:

- Options pricing engines

- Risk management systems

- Automated market makers

- Structured products and volatility derivatives

By combining autonomous execution, quantitative finance, and real-time on-chain infrastructure, Surge showcases how Sui can become the foundation for next-generation decentralized derivatives markets.

About:

***IMP: Project is Paused on X platform due X API credit limits, to test please contact us on telegram @rizwan_eth. Thank you***

- **Project Overview**

Scoop is a social execution layer for prediction markets on Sui.

Prediction markets are powerful, but most of them still live inside separate trading dashboards. At the same time, people already debate future outcomes every day on social platforms like X, Reddit, Instagram, YouTube, Telegram, and Discord. Scoop connects these two worlds by letting users trade prediction markets directly from social posts.

- **The Problem**

Until now, accessing prediction markets directly through social media was difficult.

Most prediction market venues are built around CLOB-based systems, off-chain signatures, and order matching. This makes social-native execution hard because a user comment cannot easily become a final market position without routing through order books, matching engines, signatures, and separate trading interfaces.

This creates friction for users and limits distribution for prediction market protocols.

- **Why DeepBook Predict Enables This**

DeepBook Predict changes what is possible.

Because DeepBook Predict provides programmable, on-chain prediction market primitives on Sui, Scoop can turn a simple social command into an executable market action. Instead of relying on off-chain order matching, Scoop can route user intent into DeepBook Predict, create or use a PredictManager, and mint a prediction position on-chain.

This makes social media-based prediction market access practical for the first time.

- **How Scoop Works**

The product works through a simple bot-driven flow.

Scoop posts live prediction markets on X, such as “BTC above $73,000 by expiry?” Users can reply with simple commands like “YES 5 USDC” or “NO 10 USDC.”

Scoop detects the reply, checks the user’s Sui wallet and dUSDC balance, creates or finds the user’s PredictManager, and mints the position through DeepBook Predict. Users receive a confirmation reply with transaction details and can track their positions through the Scoop dashboard.

- **Dashboard and Settlement**

Scoop also handles the post-trade experience.

The dashboard shows active markets, resolved markets, user accounts, wallet balances, open positions, resolved positions, and transaction activity. When a market settles, Scoop posts the result back into the same X thread with the closing price, outcome, redeemed positions, and transaction details.

- **Current Scope and Future Expansion**

The current implementation focuses on BTC prediction markets, but the architecture is designed to expand to more assets, more market types, and more social platforms.

Future integrations include Instagram, Reddit, YouTube, Telegram, and Discord, allowing Scoop to become a social distribution layer for prediction markets across the internet.

- **Value Proposition**

Scoop creates value for users, protocols, and communities.

Users get a simple social-native way to trade prediction markets. Protocols like DeepBook Predict get new distribution and order flow. Communities and creators can turn discussions, polls, and predictions into interactive market experiences.

- **Vision**

Scoop turns social conviction into on-chain prediction market positions.

The market is already in the comments. Scoop makes it executable.

About:

**DeepWatch **is an intelligence layer for **DeepBook Predict** where users execute all trades through a unified AI-powered trading terminal. It aggregates real-time pricing from **Polymarket **and **Kalshi **for cross-venue comparison, then uses frontier AI to transform market data into structured trading opinions. Insights are stored on **Walrus **and secured using **Seal **encryption.

**DeepBook Predict** is powerful, but its flexibility in strike selection creates decision friction. Traders hesitate, decisions slow down, and opportunities are missed. **DeepWatch **removes this friction with AI-driven execution, Auto-Trade for high-confidence signals, and a PLP-based Subscription NFT that unlocks premium insights and yield opportunities.

**Highlighted Features**

- **Cross-Venue Intelligence** – Compare DeepBook Predict, Polymarket, and Kalshi in one terminal for faster decision-making

- **Live across DeepBook markets** –Spot and Margin live on Sui Mainnet, with Predict integrated through Testnet

- **Frontier AI Reasoning** – AI converts raw market data into structured trading signals with confidence scoring

- **Auto Trade Execution** – Deploy capital automatically on high-conviction AI-identified opportunities

- **Secure Insight Layer (Walrus + Seal)** – Premium AI insights are encrypted and access-controlled on-chain

- **Stake for Access & Yield** – Subscription NFTs unlock insights while generating yield from PLP utility

**Project Structure**

**DeepWatch **was developed during the **Sui Overflow Hackathon** for the DeepBook track and comprises 4 main modules:

- **Frontend (/frontend) **- Next.js-based unified trading terminal for DeepBook Predict. Handles cross-venue market comparison, AI insight triggers + display, Simple + Advanced trading modes, Auto-Trade execution, PLP staking + Subscription NFT access, and wallet integration.

- **Sui Contracts (/contracts)** - Sui Move contracts with PLP staking pool + lending, non-transferable `Subscription` NFT, yield distribution for stakers, `seal_approve` access gate, and PLP collateral vault.

- **Walrus & Seal **- Decentralized storage and encrypted access. Walrus stores AI insight batches (single-blob format: plaintext preview + AES-256-GCM payload + Seal-wrapped key). Seal gates the encrypted slice via the on-chain `seal_approve` move call.

- **Data Integration Layer** -Polymarket + Kalshi public APIs, CoinMarketCap API, Tatum Storage API for Walrus upload.

**From Prototype to Protocol**

**DeepWatch **started as a broad AI trading prototype during the hackathon. Early versions fell into two traps:

- **Too noisy** — a full AI dashboard that fired signals everywhere.

- **Too limited** — local AI inference that hurt accuracy and usefulness.

After two major pivots, we landed on a simple truth:

**`Traders don't need more AI output — they need something simple and better decisions.`

That insight shaped **DeepWatch **into a focused intelligence layer for **DeepBook Predict:** not a firehose, not a toy, but a clear bridge from cross-venue signals to actionable execution.

About:

**VoltEdge is a professional trading desk for DeepBook Predict** — Sui's new on-chain Bitcoin prediction market. It makes an invisible market readable, fair, and trustworthy.

It does four things, in plain terms:
• a **fair-price engine** that re-computes every bet and proves it matches the blockchain exactly — 48/48 quotes, zero difference, re-runnable live in your browser;
• a **mispricing radar** that catches prices breaking the rules of math — and has already flagged real issues in the live feed (cross-tier price gaps, a stale feed, settlements up to 8.7h late);
• an **autonomous market-neutral bot** trading on testnet right now, with an on-chain receipt for every trade;
• a **Monte-Carlo risk dashboard** for the community liquidity vault.

Built on a bit-exact integer mirror of the protocol's pricing, validated by **354 tests** and a **2,620-cycle backtest** (+7.7% edge per $1 after spread). Live on Sui testnet, fully open-source.

- https://zaelbllclkxjgibdswrj.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/media/d6fb4d93-be94-46e5-bd8c-bdfef29d1bda/d1c2c4a7-4d7a-476b-aac2-fc50e301d6f5.png

About:

**TETHRA**

*Earn, lend, and borrow on DeepBook Predict, from a single trustless deposit.*

Providing liquidity on DeepBook Predict is hard work, and the position you earn stays locked inside one protocol. Tethra fixes both. One deposit into a trustless Move vault earns on Predict and gives you tPLP, a tokenized share you can also lend and borrow against across Sui DeFi. So it is not a single PLP vault but a three-tier composable stack: earn on Predict, lend on DeepBook Margin, and borrow dUSDC against your share in a self-redeeming market, all live on Sui testnet.

**PROBLEMS WE SOLVE**

The DeepBook Predict track names these problems directly. Tethra solves each one.

1. Being a PLP is hard and active, not passive. You have to price every strike against a live volatility surface, size your exposure so one bad tail does not wipe you out, and redeem your positions once they settle. Tethra turns all of that into one deposit, with no operator and no custody.

2. A PLP position is locked inside one protocol. It is not portable and cannot be used as collateral anywhere else. This is the track's headline integration ask, and it is the biggest thing we do. tPLP is a tokenized PredictManager share, and we built an isolated market where you can borrow against it, composed with DeepBook Margin. A PLP position becomes a portable, composable asset across Sui DeFi.

3. Outside LPs cannot tell whether PLP is safe. There is no live view of the volatility surface, the per-oracle exposure, or the worst-case payout. Tethra ships all three, driven by an SVI engine that matches the protocol's on-chain price to five decimal places. This covers idea-bank items 9 and 10.

4. Settled positions have to be redeemed by someone. Tethra runs a keeper that watches the public indexer and redeems on-chain in real transactions, keeping every depositor's value current. This is idea-bank item 8.

5. LP economics should be auditable and composable, not siloed. Every number in Tethra is read live from chain, and the vault composes across three protocols: Predict, DeepBook Margin, and our own borrow market.

The bigger picture: Tethra is not three separate tools. It is one composable product that turns Predict PLP into a portable, borrowable, auditable asset. The keeper, the surface viewer, and the risk dashboard are the operations and analytics layer around it, not the whole project.

**WHAT WE BUILT**

Tier 1, the Predict vault. A trustless Move vault that supplies risk-managed PLP liquidity to the live DeepBook Predict pool and values it at a conservative cost-basis floor. You deposit dUSDC and receive tPLP, a tokenized share that tracks your slice of the pool. The fee is ten percent, charged on profit only. Ninety percent of every gain stays with depositors and there is no management fee.

Tier 2, margin lending. The vault lends on DeepBook Margin and routes its deposits through a DeepBook supply referral, reclaiming the fifty percent referral slice of the spread the protocol would otherwise keep. A keeper compounds that fee straight back to depositors, so it is extra yield rather than a charge. It supports a SUI pool and a DBUSDC pool.

Tier 3, borrow against your shares. tPLP is a real token, so we built an open, isolated lending market for it. You can supply dUSDC to earn interest from borrowers, or lock your tPLP as collateral and borrow dUSDC against it, up to fifty percent loan to value, with liquidation at eighty percent. The liquidation is self-redeeming. If a loan goes bad, the market redeems the borrower's tPLP back through the Tethra vault to repay the debt. No oracle, no external liquidity, and no liquidator capital are needed.

Risk and analytics. The vault is priced by our own SVI options engine. It matches the protocol's live on-chain ask to within five decimal places, a median error of about one part in a hundred thousand. We expose this engine in three live views. A volatility surface viewer with butterfly and calendar no-arbitrage checks. A PLP risk dashboard with worst-case payout coverage and a what-if stress slider. And a per-oracle exposure breakdown that re-prices every open position through the surface under a BTC move. An analytics page shows the backtest that sets our risk parameters.

Keeper. A keeper service watches the public predict-server indexer for settled positions and calls predict::redeem_permissionless to redeem them on-chain in real transactions, keeping every depositor's value current. A second keeper handles Tier 3 liquidations. Both run from one command.

**WHY SUI, AND HOW IT FITS THE TRACK**

Tethra is built on Sui primitives. Assets are Move objects. The vault share tPLP is a first-class coin. Deposits, borrows, and liquidations bundle their steps into Programmable Transaction Blocks that execute atomically.

It meets the DeepBook Predict track minimum requirements. It integrates the Predict contract on testnet. It works end to end: deposit, keeper redeem, and withdraw. And it ships simulation results.

It also delivers three items from the track idea bank: the settled-redeem keeper, the SVI volatility surface viewer, and the PLP risk dashboard. And it answers the track's integration ask directly. tPLP is a tokenized PredictManager share that plugs into the wider Sui DeFi ecosystem as margin collateral, composed with deepbook_margin.

**WHAT IS LIVE**

Everything is live on Sui testnet and verifiable on-chain right now. All three tiers are deployed and seeded with real positions. The deposit and withdraw round trip works. The keeper has submitted real redeem transactions. The borrow market holds a real supply and borrow position. The project is ready to redeploy on mainnet day one.

**EVIDENCE**

The risk parameters come from a reproducible backtest over real BTC oracle flow, not a promised yield. House edge is about 3.8 percent. The worst drawdown was a third of one percent. The net yield after the ten percent fee was just under seven percent. The SVI model matched the on-chain ask to five decimal places across almost eighteen hundred positions. The backtest's main lesson shaped the design: the real tail risk is a rally, not a crash, so the vault controls risk with hard exposure caps instead of promising a large number.

About:

**Description**

Predict Club is a consumer-grade prediction platform where any user can trade binary options and predict crypto prices every 15 mins. This is similar to Polymarket style binary markets but better without fragmented liquidity and a fully onchain orderbook.
All Powered by DeepBook Predict on Sui.

**Problem Statement**

1. **Complexity** : Binary options are powerful but with strike prices, expiry calendars, premium. Even experienced traders get lost.

2. **Trading UX **: Current trading platforms are built for professional traders and very complex for daily user. There's no simple gamefied app that abstracts complexity from UX.

3. **No Daily Habit Loop** : DeFi has no daily ritual. No reason to open the app tomorrow. Users trade once and disappear.

**Features**

1. **Simple Interface : **Predict Club takes DeepBook's binary options infrastructure and turns it into a simple market experience: choose UP or DOWN. No complex trading flow, no order management, no manual settlement.

2. **15-minute rounds : **A new prediction round opens every 15 minutes. The oracle selects the market, the strike price is set from the live spot feed, and the countdown begins. When the round ends, settlement happens on-chain and winnings are credited automatically.

3. **Built for daily use : **Predict Club is designed to become a daily market habit. Every prediction earns XP, daily tasks keep users engaged, and weekly leaderboards with real prize pools give players a reason to return again and again.

**Future Vision**

1. **Launching with DeepBook on Mainnet** : DeepBook Predict is currently on Sui testnet, and Predict Club is live on testnet alongside it. Once DeepBook Predict launches on Sui Mainnet, we plan to bring Predict Club to mainnet as soon as possible.

2. **The Wordle of Crypto** : A daily ritual millions want but DeFi never delivered. Log in, predict BTC in 15 minutes, earn XP, climb the leaderboard. Same time tomorrow. Every day.
3. **Expansion to other platform : **Predictions should feel as easy as checking a message. A Telegram Mini App and later on a native mobile app bring Predict Club to your pocket, open, tap, predict, done.

About:

**Sui Nexus is a next‑gen decentralized knowledge canvas built on the Sui blockchain — a place where facts become permanent, upgradeable digital objects, disputes are resolved by community staking votes, and ideas interconnect through transaction‑backed edges. It’s not just an app, it’s a living, evolving ledger of truth.**

### 🚀 What Sui Nexus Is

- **Core Identity:** A **decentralized application (dApp)** running on the **Sui blockchain**.

- **Architecture:**

- **Move smart contracts** for logic and governance.

- **DAO curation** for community‑driven moderation.

- **Ledger explorer & terminal** for transparency and developer interaction.

- **Knowledge Canvas:** Users can **assert scholarly or technical facts** as **Sui Objects** — permanent yet dynamically upgradeable.

### 🔑 Key Features

- **Immutable Knowledge:** Every fact is stored as a blockchain object, ensuring permanence.

- **Dynamic Upgrades:** Facts aren’t frozen — they can evolve with new information while retaining provenance.

- **Concept Connections:** Transaction‑backed edges link ideas, creating a **graph of knowledge**.

- **Dispute Resolution:** Conflicts are settled via **staking votes**, making consensus transparent and decentralized.

- **DAO Governance:** Community curation ensures quality and prevents misinformation.

- **Developer Tools:** Includes **Sui terminal** and **ledger explorer** for technical deep dives.

### 🌍 Potential Use Cases

Domain and How Sui Nexus Helps

**Academia & Research : **Publish peer‑reviewed facts as immutable objects; track evolving theories transparently

**Open source Development : **Document technical standards, protocols, and upgrades with community consensus.

**Decentralized Journalism : **Store verified news claims, link sources, and resolve disputes via staking.

**Knowledge Graphs : **Build interconnected webs of concepts for AI training or semantic search.

**Education : **Create a blockchain‑backed encyclopedia where students and teachers co‑curate facts.

**Governance & Policy : **Transparent record of decisions, debates, and evolving regulations.

### ⚡ Why It’s Cool

- **Zero‑Cost Truth Infrastructure:** Anyone can contribute without centralized gatekeepers.

- **Collaborative Curation:** Knowledge is shaped by the community, not dictated by institutions.

- **Future‑Proof:** As facts evolve, Sui Nexus adapts — no more outdated textbooks or static wikis.

- **Trust Layer for AI:** AI systems can query Nexus for **verifiable, auditable knowledge**.

### 🧠 The Big Vision

Sui Nexus isn’t just another blockchain app — it’s a **living library of human knowledge**, decentralized, dispute‑resistant, and infinitely extensible. Imagine Wikipedia fused with GitHub, powered by blockchain consensus and DAO governance.

That’s the **Nexus**: a place where facts flow, evolve, and connect into a global truth network.

About:

**The core problem DeepFeed solves:** DeepBook is genuinely powerful , it's Sui's native, institutional-grade order book , but it has zero end-user interface. Nobody can just "go use DeepBook." It's infrastructure other apps are meant to build on top of. If you wanted to trade on it directly today, you'd be looking at raw contract calls or a developer-only SDK, with no visual order book, no plain-English explanations, nothing a normal person could use.

**What we actually built:**

- A real, usable trading interface on top of that infrastructure  live order book, live chart, clean UI

- An AI assistant that doesn't just chat *about* trading , it can actually *execute* a real conditional order for you, in plain English, with real on-chain consequences

- A genuinely novel automation layer: "buy when price drops below X" is something **no DeepBook frontend currently offers**  you built real infrastructure for this (a backend watcher, a security model using TradeCap delegation instead of giving up custody, deposit/withdraw flows).

### **What DeepFeed Does**

DeepFeed is that front door , plus something DeepBook doesn't have at all: **AI-driven conditional execution.**

- **Live order book & chart**: real DeepBook testnet data (SUI/DBUSDC and DEEP/DBUSDC), visualized clearly instead of raw numbers

- **Plain-English order placement**: every order explains what it will actually do before you submit it

- **AI assistant that can act, not just talk**: ask it to "buy 2 SUI when it drops below $3.10," and it places a real conditional order via tool-calling (powered by Groq / Llama 3.3 70B)

- **Real automated execution**: a backend watcher service monitors live prices and submits genuine signed transactions to DeepBook when your conditions are met, even while you're offline

- **Non-custodial by design**: your funds live in your own DeepBook BalanceManager. DeepFeed's execution wallet only ever holds a TradeCap (trading rights), never ownership it can place orders on your behalf, but it can never withdraw your funds. Only your own wallet can do that.

### **How It Works**

- Connect your Sui wallet

- One-time setup: create a BalanceManager and grant DeepFeed a TradeCap (you keep full ownership)

- Deposit SUI/DBUSDC into your BalanceManager

- Place a conditional order  manually, or just ask the AI assistant

- A backend service watches DeepBook prices every few seconds and executes your order the moment your condition is met

- Withdraw anytime, independently, with your own wallet  DeepFeed is never in the loop for that.

About:

Agent Arena is a Sui Overflow 2026 project: a  AI Agent competition layer built around DeepBook Predict.

Instead of giving agents private keys or asking them to build raw Sui transactions, Agent Arena lets external AI Agents join BTC 15-minute DeepBook Predict competitions through a platform runtime API. Owners claim and fund platform-managed Testnet trading wallets, while agents submit structured trading intents such as hold, open, reduce, and close.

In this demo:

- AI Agent pairing and owner claim flow

- Platform-managed Testnet trading wallet

- Agent runtime credential handoff

- BTC 15-minute DeepBook Predict competition

- Intent validation and guarded execution

- Leaderboard, replay timeline, risk decisions, and Predict transaction evidence

Agent Arena does not create a new prediction market. DeepBook Predict remains the market, pricing, position, and settlement layer. Agent Arena provides the competition rules, wallet management, execution guardrails, scoring, replay, and agent-readable skill surface.

Built for Sui Overflow 2026.

About:

Liquidation on DeepBook Margin pays a bot roughly five percent of your collateral. A stop-loss can't help. It only sells, never repays debt, and DeepBook's own documentation specifies that the first step of liquidation is to cancel every open order on your account. There is a structural deficiency in the standard safety tool.

Guardian is the protection layer that actually works. It watches your real risk ratio, which keeps decaying from interest even on a flat day where a price-based stop-loss can't see what's coming, and deleverages your position before you cross the liquidation line. It cancels risky orders, repays debt from idle balance, and sells small capped tranches to repay more. Every action is on-chain and reduce-only by construction. No Guardian function can move your funds to anyone except you.

When a crash is too fast for the ladder, Guardian's white knight triggers. It self-liquidates your position and routes the five percent rescue reward back to your wallet, instead of a bot's. Even our failure mode pays you.

Non-custodial. Reduce-only. Deployed on Sui testnet, with eighteen passing contract tests including the white-knight float invariant.

About:

**Squall - structured yield on Sui, built on volatility.**

Squall is the Ribbon Finance of Sui: tokenized ERC-4626 vaults on DeepBook Predict, powered by the first on-chain volatility index on Sui, with a verifiable track record on Walrus.

**The problem**

The first DeFi option vaults (Ribbon, Friktion) did billions, then collapsed in 2022. They sold options too cheap in weekly auctions and ran unhedged, so one crash wiped out LPs.

**What Squall does**

- **Earn the spread, not a cheap auction.** The vault is a liquidity provider on DeepBook Predict's vol-surface-priced order book. It becomes the house, collecting the premium from real, recurring trading flow.

- **Hedged by design.** A tail overlay caps the crash that killed the old vaults.

- **Provable, not promised.** Every vault action writes a hash-chained NAV snapshot to Walrus, anchored on-chain in a ProofLog, so anyone can re-fetch and verify the record.

**Three firsts on Sui**

The first on-chain volatility index (a shared object any protocol can read), full ERC-4626 vault semantics in Move (with virtual-offset

inflation protection), and a hash-chained, on-chain-anchored proof record.

**Live & verifiable on Sui testnet**

Full cycle verified end-to-end against the real DeepBook Predict: deposit 100 DUSDC -> allocate into Predict PLP -> harvest + NAV report ->

redeem, plus a live vol-index update and Walrus snapshots re-verified in the Proof tab.

- Package: 0x6db7afe5caa78f6c1caedf6546b44af1b1bdc35f6f4f8f3062e3b675f7396d3f

- Vault:

0xbc279cb0ce8622b5e27c787961b7b39a55ebea0cf6ad993bdea6a43bc55f3d9c

- VolIndex: 0x7521737597f1697c18cd4382a5ff43d62b89cef3667d1d8d02e48cdda9d67f0c

**Risk & backtesting (honest)**

Seeded simulation of the strategy logic: the hedge cut max drawdown from ~19% to ~16.5%, and through a real 51% BTC crash held drawdown to ~20%. Illustrative, reproducible, not a yield guarantee. You can lose money.

**Built on**

DeepBook Predict (vol-priced order book + OracleSVI) and Walrus (proof storage). Move on Sui, a TypeScript keeper, and a Next.js app, all live on testnet.

About:

DeepBook Predict launched on-chain BTC options priced by a live volatility surface, settled on Sui — but there was no way to actually trade it: just raw RPC and a REST endpoint. Tidecast is the desk.

Most front-ends that have appeared on Predict are web or Telegram apps that wrap it as a bet button, a chat assistant, or a payoff-curve builder. Tidecast is built for whoever prices the trade:

• The volatility surface, drawn — a live SVI vol smile and a per-strike positioning heatmap, sharing one strike axis with the ladder. Hover a strike and the smile marker, the heat bar and the order ticket all track it. The surface is the product, not a number hidden behind a quote.

• A native desk, not a web page — Tauri desktop, in-process Ed25519 signing, no wallet popup, no browser chrome. The density and latency of a real trading terminal.

• Real and checkable — gas-free devInspect quotes (priced against the same SVI surface Predict settles on) and a real testnet mint you can open on Suiscan. No mock counterparty, no placeholder charts.

The trade loop: pick a rolling hourly expiry → strike ladder around spot with each side's implied win probability → live quote that re-prices as you type → one click builds and signs the PTB (deposit + mint) in-process → the position drops into the dock with live PnL → one-click redeem on expiry.

Tidecast ships no Move package of its own — it is a pure integration: the on-chain logic is DeepBook Predict, called through its public entry functions.

Built with Tauri 2.0 + Vite/React 19 + HeroUI v3, @mysten/sui for devInspect quotes and PTB writes.

About:

**Nanawise — DeepBook Predict, Simple Enough for Your Grandma**

**Track:** DeepBook Predict · **Chain:** Sui Testnet (predict-testnet-4-16)

Nanawise is a chat-first, AI-powered client for DeepBook Predict. Instead of learning prediction-market jargon, installing wallets, buying gas, and figuring out strikes and expiries, users simply tell Nanawise what they think will happen. They type something like *"Bet $1 Bitcoin goes up this hour"* or send a voice note, and Nanawise reads the live volatility-surface market, constructs a valid DeepBook Predict position, and hands back a single button to execute the trade on-chain. No seed phrases, no gas, no order tickets.

**TRY IT NOW**

Telegram bot at [https://t.me/nana_wise_bot](https://t.me/nana_wise_bot) and the web/mini app at [https://nanawise.vercel.app](https://nanawise.vercel.app). Demo video at [https://youtu.be/aoIXvgXT7m8](https://youtu.be/aoIXvgXT7m8) and pitch deck at [https://nanawise.vercel.app/pitch](https://nanawise.vercel.app/pitch).

**THE PROBLEM**

DeepBook Predict already solves the protocol layer of prediction markets, with live SVI volatility surfaces, sub-hour oracle updates, PLP liquidity vaults, and dynamic strike pricing. But a second problem remains: prediction markets are still built for traders, not normal people. To place a single bet, a user has to install a wallet, store a seed phrase, acquire gas tokens, learn strikes and expiries and pricing, and somehow avoid reverting transactions along the way. The friction keeps the potential audience tiny.

**THE SOLUTION**

Nanawise removes every barrier. Where the traditional flow asks users to install a wallet and save a seed phrase, Nanawise lets them sign in with Google via zkLogin. Where the old flow makes users buy and manage gas, Nanawise sponsors it fully through Enoki. Instead of learning options terminology, users speak naturally in plain English; instead of manually choosing valid strikes, the AI selects tradeable in-band strikes; and instead of juggling multiple transactions, users get a one-tap deposit and mint.

**USER EXPERIENCE**

The whole journey fits in a few taps. A user opens the Telegram bot, runs `/start`, and signs in with Google, which creates a zkLogin wallet and a PredictManager on-chain. From there they type or speak a prediction, the AI prepares a trade, and they tap **Open — Pay X dUSDC** to make the position live. Positions are closed with `/redeem` or left to settle automatically. To test the full loop in about a minute: run `/start`, sign in, then send *"bet $1 bitcoin goes up in the next hour"* or a voice note. Need funds? Use `/faucet`. Prefer buttons? Use `/market` and pick UP $1 or DOWN $1.

**DEEPBOOK PREDICT INTEGRATION**

Nanawise uses a typed PredictClient SDK for every read and transaction it constructs. The core lifecycle covers create_manager, deposit, mint, redeem, and redeem_permissionless. Range markets are supported through mint_range, redeem_range, and range_key::new. Pricing and reads run through get_trade_amounts, get_range_trade_amounts, balance, position, and market_key::new, while the PLP vault is handled by supply and withdraw. Every trade is priced directly against the live DeepBook Predict oracle: Nanawise enforces the protocol ask bounds of 1% to 99%, prevents invalid strike selection, avoids near-expiry oracle rounds that would revert, and combines indexer data with on-chain devInspect so users only ever see valid, executable trades.

**ARCHITECTURE**

A Telegram front end (text, voice, and buttons) feeds an AI trading agent built on GPT-4o with tool-calling, which routes through a sponsor API and Enoki before settling on DeepBook Predict on Sui. The Telegram bot handles the agent, voice transcription, market previews, and position execution. A Next.js mini app covers zkLogin onboarding, deposit, mint, and redeem. The backend runs Postgres and Redis for session management and sponsored-transaction generation. A keeper service monitors settled markets and calls redeem_permissionless to settle positions for users automatically.

**SECURITY MODEL**

Nanawise is self-custodial: the zkLogin key is generated and stored in the browser, and the backend never stores user keys. The only metadata kept server-side is the public wallet address, the PredictManager ID, and session references. Gas sponsorship through Enoki is restricted to allow-listed Predict operations and bound to the authenticated user.

**AI LAYER**

GPT-4o converts a phrase like *"I think Bitcoin pumps this hour"* into a valid DeepBook Predict position expressed as (strike, side, size) and prepares it as an executable trade. GPT-4o Transcribe turns voice notes into the same trading intents.

**TRACK REQUIREMENTS**

Nanawise integrates DeepBook Predict on testnet with full binary and range lifecycle support, and delivers an end-to-end working product spanning Google sign-in, zkLogin, deposit, mint, and redeem. It includes a live PLP vault integration for the vault-strategy requirement, and every position is executed on-chain on Sui testnet.

**ON-CHAIN ADDRESSES**

Predict Package: 0xf5ea2b3749c65d6e56507cc35388719aadb28f9cab873696a2f8687f5c785138 Predict Registry: 0x43af14fed5480c20ff77e2263d5f794c35b9fab7e2212903127062f4fe2a6e64 Predict Object: 0xc8736204d12f0a7277c86388a68bf8a194b0a14c5538ad13f22cbd8e2a38028a dUSDC: 0xe95040085976bfd54a1a07225cd46c8a2b4e8e2b6732f140a0fc49850ba73e1a::dusdc::DUSDC PLP: 0xf5ea2b3749c65d6e56507cc35388719aadb28f9cab873696a2f8687f5c785138::plp::PLP Indexer: [https://predict-server.testnet.mystenlabs.com](https://predict-server.testnet.mystenlabs.com) RPC: [https://fullnode.testnet.sui.io:443](https://fullnode.testnet.sui.io:443)

**DEPLOYMENT**

Web app at [https://nanawise.vercel.app](https://nanawise.vercel.app) and Telegram bot at [https://t.me/nana_wise_bot](https://t.me/nana_wise_bot). If the mini app is between redeploys, the Telegram bot supports the complete trading flow on its own.

About:

**Predict directly from X, leverage, private trading, and agent strategies on DeepBook Predict. On web and a native iOS + Android app**.

DeepBook Predict is a great primitive (oracle-settled binary markets where the oracle decides, not a committee) but not yet a product. Yosuku is the consumer layer that makes it usable, by people, developers, and AI agents.

Tweet "*@yosukuapp *BTC up 3x" and an agent opens that exact position on-chain, from a vault only you can withdraw from. It trades your money but cannot take it: the no-divert guarantee is enforced in Move and proven on-chain (user +0.953 DUSDC, agent 0.000). That is the structural answer to the agent-drain problem.

What we built:

- A live feed, like TikTok for prediction markets: swipe through markets, one-tap UP or DOWN, or post your own take leveraging deepbook predict strike feature.

- A native mobile app (Android APK + iOS Expo TestFlight), gasless and non-custodial.

- Agent strategies with capped budgets and zero withdrawal control, with MemWal-backed verifiable memory.

- Leverage, private mode, and a one-deposit Trading Balance account.

- The first TypeScript SDK and the first MCP server for DeepBook Predict, live on npm (about 1,870 installs combined).

- An attested agent: decisions signed in an AWS Nitro enclave, verified on-chain.

Everything is on testnet, because Predict is testnet-only today. We go to mainnet the day it does.

About:

DeepBook Market OS — a market operating system built on DeepBookV3 (Sui), composing Spot, Margin, and Predict into one console: execution, accounts, liquidity, structured products, and market creation.

🔗 Live app: [https://aleksarriola-max.github.io/deepbook-market-os/](https://aleksarriola-max.github.io/deepbook-market-os/) 💻 Source: [https://github.com/aleksarriola-max/deepbook-market-os](https://github.com/aleksarriola-max/deepbook-market-os)

Everything in this demo is reading LIVE data from DeepBookV3's public mainnet indexer — no backend, no mock data. Every analytic (transaction-cost analysis, Kyle's lambda, walk-the-book impact curves, empirical fill probabilities, historical backtests, volatility cones and smiles) is computed client-side from real chain data, with full formulas documented in the repo. The handful of numbers that aren't live (Predict pricing, since Predict is still testnet-only; Vault target APRs) are clearly tagged SIMULATED on screen.

**WHAT'S INSIDE** ⚡ Smart Execution Terminal — turn a trading intent into an atomic ladder of limit orders, staged as one PTB, with live fill-probability and backtest comparisons against just crossing the spread ♟ Strategy Builder — tunable ladders with empirical touch-probability fills, historical backtesting, and a 48-combination parameter sweep that ranks ladder shapes by expected edge ∑ Execution Analytics — live TCA decomposition, Kyle's lambda with R², exact impact curves, a ≤3-hop smart-route explorer, and a per-trade cost time series ▣ Desk Manager — BalanceManager account model with live orders, volume, and on-chain audit log for any address ◈ Liquidity Vaults — a maker leaderboard computed from live order/trade data ◐ Predict Workspace — binary event market pricing via the d₁/N(d₁) formula, plus an empirical volatility smile and position delta ⬡ Structured Products — compose payoffs across Spot/Margin/Predict legs with a live-updating payoff curve ◎ Portfolio Command — load any wallet's real margin positions and stress-test them against a hypothetical price move ✦ Market Creation Console — live PoolCreated feed and generated SDK code for launching new DeepBook pools

**TECH STACK** Vite + React 19 + TypeScript · DeepBookV3 SDK (@mysten/deepbook-v3) · @mysten/sui · hand-rolled SVG charts, no chart library · static frontend deployed on GitHub Pages · optional Supabase sync for saved strategies/positions (opt-in, not required to use the app)

About:

**The compiler and IDE for DeepBook. Compile trading intent into priced, simulated, risk-scored, executable on-chain strategies.**

DeepForge is a declarative intent compiler for DeepBook. Three front-ends (natural language, a visual graph builder, and a canonical `*.deepforge.yaml` DSL) lower to a single typed **Financial IR**, which a deterministic toolchain resolves against the live on-chain **SVI volatility surface**, prices leg-by-leg through `devInspect` static execution (the exact protocol quote at zero gas), samples a **risk-neutral payoff distribution** from the protocol's own price function, and scores tail risk against live **PLP vault state**. The output compiles to **Programmable Transaction Blocks** and mints a composable, versioned, forkable on-chain **Strategy object**. It ships with a Terraform-style `plan` / `apply` lifecycle across a web IDE and a headless CLI. The language model only performs the intent to IR lowering; every financial number downstream is deterministic, unit-tested code sourced on-chain.

**The problem:**

DeepBook is genuinely powerful market structure: binary up and down positions, vertical ranges, and a pooled liquidity vault, all priced off a live volatility surface. The cost is that authoring on it is low-level and expert-only. To express a single view a user has to hand-compose strike selection, the SVI surface parameters, expiry math, PLP liquidity, exposure limits, settlement, and rollovers. That is the equivalent of writing assembly. It is error-prone, it excludes almost everyone, and there is no portable, auditable unit of "a strategy." Logic lives in throwaway scripts and notebooks instead of as a composable on-chain primitive.

**The solution:**

DeepForge raises the altitude from transactions to intent. You describe a view, and the compiler does the rest:

1. *Lower* any of three front-ends into one typed Financial IR.

2. *Resolve* strikes and expiry against the live oracle and its SVI surface.

3. *Price* every leg on-chain via `devInspect`, so the displayed cost is the exact amount the contract will charge, with no gas spent.

4. *Simulate* the full payoff distribution (best, expected, worst, probability of profit) from the same price function the vault uses, so pricing and simulation are mutually consistent.

5. *Score risk* from live vault state: liquidity, oracle freshness, tail loss, and your max-loss policy, with legs outside the mintable ask band blocked before signing.

6. *Execute* as Programmable Transaction Blocks `predict::mint` / `mint_range` / `supply`) and **mint a forkable Strategy object** that records its parent, risk score, and IR hash on-chain.

The result is a Terraform-style workflow for financial strategies: `plan` is a priced, simulated dry run, `apply` is real execution, available in both a polished web IDE and a CLI.

**How it impacts:**

- Accessibility**.** It collapses an expert, multi-step, error-prone process into a single intent, widening the funnel of who can use DeepBook.

- Correctness and trust. Cost comes from `devInspect`, the distribution comes from the protocol price function, and risk comes from live vault state, so outputs are verifiable on the explorer.

- Composability. Strategies become first-class on-chain objects: versioned, forkable, transferable, and auditable. A fork records lineage on-chain, so strategies behave like composable money-legos that other Sui protocols can read and build on. This is GitHub for trading strategies.

- Ecosystem flywheel. More compiled strategies means more on-chain volume routed through DeepBook, more PLP utilization, and a growing public library of forkable, risk-scored primitives.

**Business angle:**

- Market**.** Prediction markets and on-chain structured products are becoming real market structure. DeepForge is the authoring and distribution layer that sits on top of that primitive, the way an IDE and package registry sit on top of a language.

- Distribution and network effects**.** A public marketplace of forkable strategies compounds: every published and forked strategy increases the surface area and the value of the catalog.

- Monetization paths**.** A take rate on the strategy marketplace, fees on compiled execution volume, and premium tooling (the keeper network for auto-roll and settled-redeem, plus team and pro features in the IDE).

- Mainnet alignment**.** The entire chain layer is isolated in a single config module, so mainnet is effectively a one-config swap. This maps directly onto the Sui Overflow split-prize model, which rewards mainnet deployment.

**Live and verifiable:**

- 100 percent on-chain pricing via `devInspect`

- Confirmed `RangeMinted` events on Sui Testnet from the web app.

- DeepForge Strategy package: `0xe7432b172c7bc3be90f87a2cf7e81cd936fb737bea2109f08c68e090a1eb8ae3`

- DeepBook integration target: `0xf5ea2b3749c65d6e56507cc35388719aadb28f9cab873696a2f8687f5c785138`

**Architecture and links:**

- Pitch deck : [https://gamma.app/docs/Compile-intent-into-programmable-DeepBook-Predict-strategies-2ax9go95xw8yymj](https://gamma.app/docs/Compile-intent-into-programmable-DeepBook-Predict-strategies-2ax9go95xw8yymj)

**The takeaway.** Most teams built products that use DeepBook. DeepForge is the programming environment that makes building on DeepBook dramatically easier, and turns strategies into composable on-chain assets

About:

Prediction markets today throw away the most valuable part of a trader's view. When you believe Bitcoin will settle at $103,200 — not just "go up" — no market on any chain rewards that precision. APEX changes that.

APEX is a precision forecasting protocol built on Sui and settled through DeepBook Predict. Instead of picking a binary outcome, participants submit an exact numerical forecast. That forecast is translated into a volatility-priced range position on DeepBook Predict's on-chain settlement layer. The closer the oracle lands to your number, the higher your accuracy score and the larger your share of the prize pool.

To prevent copy-trading and last-minute herding, APEX uses a commit-reveal design. Participants first submit a cryptographic hash of their forecast — hiding their number on-chain. After the pool closes, they reveal in a single transaction. Nobody can see anyone else's forecast until it's too late to copy it.

Rewards are determined by three factors: Accuracy Score based on distance from the oracle result, Time Bonus that rewards early commitment, and Conviction Bonus that rewards participants who never change their forecast. These mechanics reward genuine information over consensus-chasing.

Every settled pool updates the participant's Forecaster Reputation Score — a live Sui object owned by their wallet. The FRS tracks rolling accuracy, pool count, streaks, and tier (OBSERVER → ANALYST → EXPERT → APEX). Because it is a standard Sui object with a public interface, any protocol on Sui can read it with a single Move call. A lending protocol can offer better rates to proven forecasters. A margin desk can offer lower collateral. Forecasting accuracy becomes portable collateral across all of Sui DeFi.

Sui stack used: DeepBook Predict for trustless oracle settlement and volatility-priced range positions. Programmable Transaction Blocks for atomic five-command commit-reveal execution with no ZK proofs required. Sui's object ownership model for the FRS composability layer. Move ownership rules for custodian-free prize escrow.

APEX is live on Sui testnet

About:

DarkPool is the canonical reference application built on DeepBook V3 + Predict. Prediction markets with native AI-agent support, a position-privacy vault, and a Move-native Optimistic Oracle; all on top of DeepBook's primitives, with nothing forked. What it proves: DeepBook V3 and Predict are production-ready enough to ship a full prediction-market product in 6 weeks order books, key-less delegation, and shared-vault liquidity all working in one stack that nothing else on any L1 can match. The four DeepBook-native pillars:

1. DeepBook V3 spot pools host every binary event market. Each YES/NO question publishes its own Pool<YES, DUSDC> + Pool<NO, DUSDC> via permissionless pool creation. Real Coin types, real on-chain order books, zero-DEEP UX (fees paid in the input token).

2. DeepBook Predict powers BTC up/down strikes. The SVI vol surface + shared PLP vault solve cold-start liquidity; markets are tradeable the second they're published. Our predict_facade adds attribution events without forking.

3. Dark-Pool Vault uses a V3 BalanceManager held by a keeper for k-anonymous trading. Depositors get receipt shares; the vault trades on their behalf via TradeCap. Live on testnet with verified mints + V3 quotes.

4. Native AI agents trade via V3 TradeCap, Move-enforced delegation without custody. Sign one personal message, get a hosted, sponsored, autonomous trader. Groq + Gemini LLM routing, Pyth-grounded reasoning, on-chain audit trail per tick. AgentOwnerCap stays in the user's wallet for pause + reclaim.

Plus a Move-native Optimistic Oracle that auto-finalizes.

DarkBook is an OTC block-trading venue on Sui: off-chain intent matching, atomic on-chain settlement through a Move vault, and a DeepBook V3 fallback so every order fills.

When two intents don't match peer-to-peer within 120s, the engine routes the unmatched order to DeepBook V3 — a side-aware market order through a BalanceManager (atomic for buys; fee-buffered for sells, since DeepBook takes the taker fee from the input asset), with proceeds swept back to the trader.

Peer matches settle atomically through a Move vault at a fair mid-price. Every order fills — peer-to-peer or on the book. Trade large blocks with no market impact — verified end-to-end on Sui testnet.

About:

- Slete is a precision price prediction market built on Sui. Instead of simply picking up or down, you predict the exact price where an asset closes, the closer you are, the bigger your share of the prize pool.

- Every market has a fixed expiry. When it settles, the oracle records the final price and payouts are distributed to the top predictors by proximity; 1st takes 60%, 2nd takes 25%, 3rd takes 15%.

Two ways to play:

- Vertical Range Markets: predict where within a price range an asset closes. Each market defines a min strike and tick size, and you pick your exact level. The full distribution of crowd predictions is visible before you commit, so you can see where consensus sits and decide whether to follow or go contrarian.

- Binary Markets: predict simply whether the asset closes above or below the previous day's close price at expiry.

- All positions are minted onchain via DeepBook on Sui Testnet, with every prediction recorded transparently. Entry is ≥1 DUSDC per prediction and prize pools grow as more predictors join each market.

Links:
- github: https://github.com/LambertAlpha/LadderVault
- youtube: https://youtu.be/qb7lspMbuXY

Media:
- None listed

About:

LadderVault is an actively risk-managed LP vault for DeepBook Predict. Passive prediction-market LPs earn fees but eat asymmetric tail losses: when traders concentrate positions on a hot strike and price pins there, those positions settle in-the-money together and the LP can't dodge the hit. LadderVault watches a concentration signal — pin_risk, the payout at the current price over total inventory — and automatically withdraws from Predict when risk spikes, re-entering (with hysteresis) once it subsides. No directional prediction; pure tail-risk management. In a faithful offline kill-test it cuts the worst-case (price-pinned) scenario from a passive LP's −13.4% to −0.1% — an 82% tail reduction approaching a look-ahead Oracle bound. The Move contracts are deployed on Sui testnet with passing unit tests and a real end-to-end transaction (deposit → 80% concentration → auto-exit), all Explorer-verifiable. The LP interface aligns to DeepBook Predict's supply/withdraw/range primitives; switching to live Predict is the first post-hackathon step.

- https://zaelbllclkxjgibdswrj.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/media/77a7521d-8a78-4ac9-b4e7-f054d6d889cd/bcccc167-18e8-4fb4-a276-47fe324240ef.png

About:

**Stratos **is a retail friendly interface for multi-leg binary options on DeepBook Predict, built for Sui Overflow 2026. DeFi options haven't scaled because retail users can't construct multi-leg strategies without quantitative finance expertise. Stratos collapses that into a single signature, and adds an LP onboarding page so retail can be the protocol's counterparty too.

Live demo: [https://stratos-sui-hackathon.vercel.app](https://stratos-sui-hackathon.vercel.app)

Source: [https://github.com/Thunderroar/Stratos](https://github.com/Thunderroar/Stratos)

**What's inside:**

- Five strategy templates (bull/bear ladder, strangle, range bet, range band) with live cost preview from on-chain get_trade_amounts, payoff diagrams, Black-Scholes Greeks, and probability of profit via Monte Carlo

- Intraday backtest against historical SVI snapshots from the oracle, using the same pricing math as the Risk Panel

- Multi-leg execution as a single atomic PTB signature. Impossible on EVM without a custom contract per strategy shape

- Our own Move executor module on Sui testnet that wraps Predict's mint calls into named, validated, event-emitting strategies. Five entry functions, one per template

- Live Activity Feed querying StrategyExecuted events directly from Sui RPC. No indexer, no backend. Strategies become first-class on-chain artifacts

- Earn page where retail can supply DUSDC to Predict's vault and be the protocol's counterparty. Same business model as Hyperliquid HLP and GMX GLP, with a UI a non-quant can actually read.

**On-chain:**

- Network: Sui Testnet

- Stratos Move package: 0x1353e53419f8c227df2e85817cf8bfbdb08c290d01f5dbdfe44058fb19dcbcfa

- Suiscan: [https://suiscan.xyz/testnet/object/0x1353e53419f8c227df2e85817cf8bfbdb08c290d01f5dbdfe44058fb19dcbcfa](https://suiscan.xyz/testnet/object/0x1353e53419f8c227df2e85817cf8bfbdb08c290d01f5dbdfe44058fb19dcbcfa)

- Module: stratos::executor

- Functions: execute_bull_ladder, execute_bear_ladder, execute_strangle, execute_range_bet, execute_range_band

- Event: StrategyExecuted

**Track: **DeepBook Specialized

Bonus track: DeFi & Payments

When DeepBook Predict reaches mainnet, Stratos becomes the structured products layer for binary options on Sui on day one. Same code, no migration. Roadmap from there: a structured options vault on top of the Earn layer with built-in tail hedging, a public on-chain vol index derived from the SVI surface that other Sui protocols can consume for pricing and risk modeling.

Built with React 19, Vite, Tailwind v4, @mysten/dapp-kit-react, @mysten/sui, Recharts, and Move on Sui.

- youtube: https://youtu.be/hgZAdGqas6A

Media:
- None listed

About:

Jaga — PLP yield minus crash insurance, in one composable token.

DeepBook Predict's PLP holders are effectively "the house": they earn premium when markets are calm but absorb brutal payouts when BTC moves violently — a fat left tail that scares serious LPs away.

Jaga fixes the risk shape. It's an automated vault that (1) supplies dUSDC to Predict's PLP pool to earn yield, and (2) each roll spends a small premium buying out-of-the-money binary DOWN options on Predict as crash insurance. When markets are calm you collect PLP yield minus a thin premium; when BTC crashes, the hedge pays off exactly when PLP bleeds. Depositors hold one composable Coin<SHARE> (jSHARE); a keeper auto-rolls the hedge every expiry.

The whole lifecycle runs end-to-end on Sui testnet against the real Predict contracts. A 30,000-path Monte-Carlo backtest shows ~16-point better CVaR-1% for ~1% of yield given up.

About:

**PolySui is a prediction market on DeepBook.**

Bet on Bitcoin going up or down in 15-minute rounds — gasless, no wallet, no seed phrase. Just sign in with Google and take a side. It's designed to be intuitive: no learning curve, no setup — anyone can jump in and play in seconds.

And you're not locked to one call. Hold up *and* down at the same time as separate positions, cash either one out before the round ends, or supply liquidity and "be the house."

It's built on DeepBook Predict, Sui's prediction protocol. Predict is powerful but built for pros — PolySui makes it something anyone can use, and brings the liquidity to back it. The odds come straight from Predict's live pricing; we just make them simple.

Everything is real and on-chain: a transparent 3% house edge on every trade, a portfolio rebuilt from the chain, and a leaderboard of real settled results that can't be faked.

Live on testnet; we redeploy on mainnet the day Predict ships there.

About:

SAGE is a full BTC trading terminal built inside Telegram, powered by DeepBook Predict on Sui testnet. It combines a conversational AI agent, live charting mini-app, real-time market intelligence, voice commands, and automated execution — all without leaving your chat.

Users get a Sui wallet created instantly on first message, with 5 dUSDC deposited automatically to start trading immediately. From there, SAGE guides you through binary options trades on BTC/USD — pick a timeframe (15min, 30min, or 1hr), pick a direction (UP or DOWN), pick your size, and confirm. SAGE shows live DeepBook implied odds on every direction button, warns you if you're going against the market consensus, and provides a volatility and risk context line powered by an SVI model before every trade. Voice notes are fully supported — speak your trade and SAGE executes it.

The built-in Telegram mini-app delivers a live BTC/USD candlestick chart with real-time price data, switchable timeframes synced to active DeepBook windows, live countdown timers on each odds pill, and DeepBook implied probabilities updating in real time.

Beyond manual trading, SAGE offers a full suite of automated features: arb auto-trade monitors the spread between Polymarket and DeepBook implied probabilities and auto-executes trades when the gap exceeds 12 percentage points, with user-defined budgets and daily trade caps. Wallet copy-trading lets users paste any Sui wallet address and mirror every DeepBook position that wallet opens, automatically, at a custom size. Trade laddering spreads a position across multiple strikes in a single command. Price alerts notify users when BTC hits a target. Daily budget caps enforce loss limits. Positions auto-settle at expiry with automatic claiming.

SAGE also delivers a morning brief at 9am UTC with market context, tracks win streaks, and maintains a global leaderboard ranking all traders by net profit. In group chats, SAGE responds when @mentioned, making it usable as a shared trading intelligence bot for communities. Every position includes a Suiscan explorer link, and all settlement is fully on-chain via DeepBook's oracle system.

About:

HELIX is a prediction market on DeepBook Predict where what you believe becomes a real, on-chain position — without needing to understand options.

You sign in with Google (zkLogin — no seed phrase), then say what you think about Bitcoin with a few sliders: direction, confidence, horizon, volatility. An AI compiler prices that against DeepBook Predict's live SVI surface, builds the best-fit structure (directional, range, or bracketed bet), and shows you the payoff and the real risks — stale data, thin liquidity, time to resolution — in plain language, before you sign. One confirmation mints it through `predict::mint` as a Sui object you own, verifiable on-chain.

Because strategies are real objects, two things become possible that don't exist elsewhere. You can attach a private thesis, encrypted with Seal and stored on Walrus — structure and performance stay public, your reasoning unlocks only for paying copiers (verified across two wallets: owner reads it, a stranger is denied). And strong strategies can be bred — crossing management style into a new generation with a verifiable parent→child lineage. A track record that can't be faked because it lives on the chain.

Live on Sui testnet: published + upgraded Move package composing the real DeepBook Predict contract (mint / supply / redeem / PredictManager), `/compile` against the live BTC oracle, real mints on Suiscan, Seal + Walrus + zkLogin all working. Tests: Move 64 / TEE 20 / Indexer 7.

About:

VolShape Studio is a payoff-first trading interface for DeepBook Predict on Sui testnet.

Instead of asking users to choose strikes and legs first, VolShape lets users draw the terminal payoff curve they want. The system compiles that curve into the closest executable DeepBook Predict positions, explains the tradeoff between the drawn target and executable approximation, routes execution through a connected Sui wallet, and tracks resulting positions in Portfolio.

For the DeepBook track, the core contribution is a new execution experience around DeepBook Predict: draw desired outcome, compile to executable positions, sign with wallet, and redeem at expiry.

About:

DeepMarket: Open, verifiable prediction markets on Sui.

Today's prediction markets ask you to trust a company. Funds get frozen, accounts get geoblocked, and worst of all markets resolve to the wrong answer when a handful of whales control the oracle. In 2025 alone, users lost millions on markets like the "Zelenskyy suit" and "UFO disclosure" bets that settled against plain reality.

DeepMarket fixes this by putting the entire market on-chain... pricing, trading, and settlement so every outcome is provable and no one can pause your withdrawal or override the result.

What you can do:

- Back any outcome. Not just yes/no any-N markets (like "Who wins the 2026 World Cup?"), where each outcome is its own token traded on a real order book.

- Predict price. Mint UP, DOWN, or range options on a live BTC oracle SVI-priced and settled trustlessly on-chain.

- Be the house. Supply the PLP vault to earn the premium traders pay, with a live NAV and risk dashboard.

- Let agents trade. Autonomous AI agents trade under an on-chain capability every decision is public, verifiable, and tied to its reasoning.

Built Sui-native, end to end:

- Move smart contracts for markets, the parimutuel vault, and settlement, markets are shared objects anyone can trade against, and Move's resource model makes tokens impossible to forge or double-spend.

- DeepBook V3 as the central limit order book, the reason we're on Sui. Our outcome tokens trade on real, shared on-chain liquidity instead of a bootstrapped AMM.

- On-chain BTC oracle feeding Predict options, with Sui's sub-second finality settling them the instant they expire.

- AgentCap: a Move capability that gates which AI agents can trade and makes every action attributable on SuiScan.

- Walrus stores agent memory using memwal, verifiable, with blob hashes anchored on Sui.

Live on Sui testnet — trade from the web app or straight from our Telegram bot (t.me/sui_deepMarket_bot).
